# Fiesta 2016 server reconstruction: overview, index and bug register (X01)

Entry point of the behavioural specification reconstructed from the 2016 server binaries (`Files/Server/2016`). The
per-subsystem documents (`login-server.md`, `login/`, `wm/`, `zone/`, `db/`, `shared/`) hold the pseudocode; this file
ties them together: which process does what, which messages a feature crosses, the vocabulary, the statements later
items corrected, and every behaviour of the 2016 servers that looks unintended.

Evidence levels as in `AGENTS.md`: plain = read in the 2016 code (2016 server PDB names count as code),
*(captured)*, *(pdb2017)*, *(fh-doc)*, *(db-backup)*, *(inferred)*.

> Status: X01 done (parts 1–9, `docs/plan/status/X01.md`). Brought up to date by X03 (`docs/plan/status/X03.md`) with
> X02's confidence review and its follow-ups Z31, Z32, W06, D08: corrections in §5, bugs in §6, documents in §1, flows
> in §3, open items in §7. Brought up to date by X04 (`docs/plan/status/X04.md`, done) with P02's capture
> batches 2 and 3: corrections in §5 (§13 rows "→ X01" / "→ X04"), *(captured)* evidence and P02-B1 … B32 in §6,
> captured flows and order corrections in §3 (new §3.31), capture-validation citations renumbered (§7 → §11 … §11 → §15).
> Brought up to date by X06 (`docs/plan/status/X06.md`, done) with P02's capture batch 4 (`s37b`, `s38`, `s24`;
> capture-validation §22–§25): 13 corrections in §5 (§13 rows "→ X06"), P02-B33 … B40 and *(captured)* evidence in §6
> (register written by `docs/plan/status/X06-register.py`), captured flows and the quit / Kickout / auto-save corrections
> in §3 (new §3.32); counts and left-out rows in `docs/knowledge/X06.md`.

## 1. Reading guide

Where each behaviour is documented. Read this section and §2 first, then the document of the subsystem; check §5 for
the place you read before you rely on an older file. Every pseudocode document has the skeleton of `FORMAT.md`
(numbered §, `@ FUN_…` per block, an "Original-server bugs" table, "Not reconstructed", "Coverage boundary"); a
reference `<doc> §n` in this file and in the item files means that numbered section. Outline of a big file:
`python3 -I tools/protocol-re/sect.py <file>`; one section: `sect.py <file> <§>`.

## 2. Architecture overview

Sources: `docs/knowledge/K01.md` (topology, config; the note was `knowledge/X03.md` before the work item X03 existed),
W01 §1–§2, W05 §1, §4, L01 §1–§3, `login-server.md` §1–§2, Z01 §1, §3.3, §10, Z10 §1, S02 §1, D01 §1, D02 §1, D05 §1,
§7, D08 §1–§4, and the shipped `Files/Server/2016` config.

### 2.1 Processes

| Process | 2016 binary | ServerInfo type | Instances | Role | Database |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Login | `Login/3LoginServer2.exe` | 4 | one for the service | version check, account login, world list, world select ticket, one-time tokens | none (through Account / AccountLog) |
| Account | `Account/Account Release.exe` | 0 | one | account check, block / auth flags, PC-bang check, web key, reports | `Account` (ODBC type 0) |
| AccountLog | `AccountLog/AccountLog Release.exe` | 1 | one | login / world-select / logout records, IP block, statistics | `AccountLog` + `StatisticsData` (types 1, 2) |
| WorldManager (WM) | `w00_WorldManager/4WorldManagerServer2.exe` | 5 | **one per world** | world login, character select, zone routing, character cache, party, raid, guild, academy, friends, chat routing, KQ, OPTool | none (through Character) |
| Zone | `w00_Zone0n/5ZoneServer2.exe` (4 identical copies) | 6 | up to 12 per world | one process per zone number; simulates the maps assigned to it | none (through Character, GameLog) |
| Character | `w00_Character/Character Release.exe` | 2 | one per world | characters, items, quests, skills, guild, friends, mini-house, pets: every game-data read and write | `w00_Character` (type 10) |
| GameLog | `w00_GameLog/GameLog Release.exe` | 3 | one per world | write-only game log; hourly text log for the data warehouse | `w00_GameLog` (type 11) |
| OPTool | not shipped | 8 | optional | operator tool; connects to Login, WM, zones and the DataServers | – |

A world is exactly one WM with its zones, Character and GameLog server; a second world duplicates those and shares
Login, Account and AccountLog (K01 §1, §3). The four DataServers (Account, AccountLog, Character, GameLog) are one
framework with different parser classes and SQL wrappers (D01 §1, D05 §7; process layer and per-build differences D08 §1–§4). Login, WM and Zone link the shared network
library (S02).

Start-up dependencies (K01 §7 for the order an operator uses):

- **DataServers** listen at once. The Character server opens its link to the GameLog server first and refuses
  incoming server links until it is up (D02 knowledge, D02 §1).
- **Login** opens the OPTool listener, connects to Account and AccountLog, and opens the WM and client listeners
  once both links are connected (L01 §1.3).
- **WM** opens the OPTool listener, connects to Account, AccountLog, Character, GameLog and Login, and opens the zone
  and client listeners only after the Character link has loaded the guild and academy data (link states 5 … 0x18,
  then 1: on 0x9804 when no academy rank balance is due, `dAggregationDate` 0 included, otherwise on 0x9806 after the
  0x9805 balance; W05 §1.3–§1.4, W03 §9.1; *(captured)* 0x9804 path, capture-validation §17.3, §5.3). The Login link is not required for that.
- **Zone**: `WinMain` → service thread → the Lua start script downloaded from a certification server
  (127.0.0.1:58492, fallback `cert1.fo.gamigo:80`); the script runs every data-load stage, then every network
  service stage (Z10 §1, §7). The zone then connects to the WM, the Character server ("GameDB") and GameLog, and to
  the next zone of the ring (Z01 §1, §10). On the WM's 0x0803 it registers its maps (0x180F) and instance maps
  (0xA419) (Z01 §3.3).
- **World status**: Login shows a world as unselectable until its WM has registered with 0x0802; the WM's 0x0808
  user counts (at once and every 30 s) then update the status byte against the load table (L01 §3). The first count
  is `{0, 0}` (`MaxOfUser` still 0, cause *(inferred)*), which Login maps to `WMSUS_FULL`: world selects get "Server is
  full" for up to 30 s after every WM start ((original bug)
  P02-B22; *(captured)*, capture-validation §16.3).

### 2.2 Links

```text
game client ──1──► Login ──────► Account (9000), AccountLog (9311)
     │  ◄── WM address + validate key (0x0C0C)
     ├─────2──► WM ─────────► Login (9011), Account, AccountLog, Character (9411), GameLog (9511)
     │  ◄── zone address (0x1003)
     └─────3──► Zone n ─────► WM (9111), Character "GameDB" (9411), GameLog (9511),
                              Zone n+1 ring listener (the last zone → Zone 0)
                Character ──► GameLog (9511)
OPTool ───────► Login, WM, zones, DataServers

arrow = connecting side → listener; ports = shipped world-0 config; client links 1 → 2 → 3 in that order
```

| Connects (TCP client) | Listener (ServerInfo row, shipped port) | Carries | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|
| game client | Login (type 4, peer 20: 9010) | version check, login, world list / select, OTP login (dept 3 USER, dept 2 MISC) | `login-server.md` §4 |
| game client | WM (type 5, peer 20: 9110), address from Login's 0x0C0C | world login, character list / create / delete / select; in game: chat, party, raid, guild, friends, KQ, options, … | W01 §3–§5, W02–W04 |
| game client | zone (type 6, peer 20: 9210 / 9212 / 9214 / 9216), address from the WM's 0x1003 | everything on the map: movement, combat, items, NPCs, quests, trade, … | Z01–Z30 |
| Login | Account (type 0: 9000) | password check 0x0C5B / 0x0C5C and the other AccountDB requests | `login-server.md` §5.1, D01 §3 |
| Login | AccountLog (type 1: 9311) | IP block 0x0C5D / 0x0C5E, login records 0x0720 / 0x0721, NC_SYSLOG 0xC801 / 0xC802 | `login-server.md` §5.2, D01 §4 |
| WM | Login (type 4, peer 5: 9011) | 0x0808 user counts; world select 0x0C0D → WM, 0x0C0E back; one-time tokens 0x0C35 / 0x0C36 | `login-server.md` §5.3, W01 §3.1, §8 |
| WM | Account (9000) | world-login check 0x0C60 / 0x0C61, logout 0x0C11 (a no-op in the Account server, D01 knowledge), PC-bang check 0x082F / 0x0830, web key, reports | W01 §3, §8, D01 §3 |
| WM | AccountLog (9311) | last-logout day 0x0C62 / 0x0C63, logout 0x0722, NC_SYSLOG 0xC803 / 0xC811 | W01 §2, §3.3, D01 §4 |
| WM | Character (type 2, world: 9411) | avatars 0x0C12 / 0x0C13, create / delete / rename, character load 0x1004 → 0x1005 + blobs, login 0x1036, logout 0x1037; guild, academy, friends, options, prison data | W01 §6, W03, W04; D02 §3–§5, D04 §3–§9 |
| WM | GameLog (type 3, world: 9511) | log records | W01 §8, D05 |
| zone | WM (type 5, peer 6: 9111) | map registration 0x180F; character hand-off 0x101C → blobs → 0x101D, ticket 0x104B, map change 0x180B … 0x180E, 0x0C31; kicks 0x0C16 / 0x0C19; time 0x0821; dept 16 saves into the WM cache; mirrors of party / raid / guild / academy, chat relay, KQ | W01 §7, Z01 §5–§9, Z08 |
| zone | Character "GameDB" (9411) | ITEMDB (dept 13), CHARSAVE (dept 16), quests (17), skills (18), and the zone-side DB requests of every other subsystem | Z05, Z01 §8.4, D02–D04, D07 |
| zone | GameLog (9511) | NC_LOG_GAME / NC_LOG (dept 1), NC_SYSLOG (dept 50) | D05 |
| zone n | zone n + 1, ring listener (type 6, peer 6: 9218 … 9221); the last zone connects to zone 0 | requests that need "any zone": player search / summon / kick, EXP / item bonus events, wedding partner, instance and job dungeons, guild and academy storage, holy-promise cen transfer | Z01 §10, Z14 §2–§4, Z30 §6 |
| Character | GameLog (9511) | log records of DB-side changes (items, wedding, class change, login / logout) | D02 knowledge, D03 knowledge, D05 |
| OPTool | Login 9015, WM 9115, zone 9211 / 9213 / 9215 / 9217 (all bound to 127.0.0.1); DataServers through their single listener | dept 10 OPTOOL: status, user limits, kicks, notices | `login-server.md` §5.4, W04 §4, Z01 §3.3, D01 §2, D05 §5 |

The zones never talk to Login, Account or AccountLog; the WM does that for them (for example the PC-bang check
0x082F carries the zone's handle, D01 knowledge). The client is connected to the WM and to one zone at the same time
in game (it sends WM requests from the map, W01 §5); to Login only before the world login.

### 2.3 Common link behaviour

- **Framing and cipher**: every link uses the same frames (`[u8 len]` or `[0][u16 len]`, then `u16 opcode`, data;
  S02 §1, `docs/protocol/README.md` §1.1). Only client → server traffic is XOR-encrypted, with the 499-byte table from
  the seed 0x0807 that Login, WM and zone send right after accept (§5.1). Server links are not encrypted.
- **Server handshake**: the listener sends 0x0801 `S2SCONNECTION_RDY`; the connecting side answers 0x0802
  `{u8 echo, u8 server_id_to, u8 server_id_from, u8 world, u8 zone, u16 key = to + from}`; the listener checks
  `server_id_to` (its own type) and the key and answers 0x0803 `{u8 echo, u16 result}` (W01 §2.1, Z01 §3.3, D01 §2).
  The WM also compares a zone's IP with its ServerInfo row and does not check the world byte (W05 §5.2, W05-B13).
  Account and AccountLog do not check the world either, which is what lets them serve every world (K01 §3).
- **Handler guard**: every handler except the handshake and heartbeat first checks that its session completed the
  handshake (`CheckConnectionValidation` / `CheckAlive`). Failing it closes the link in Login and WM (L01 §4.4,
  W05 §4.5) and drops the packet in the DataServers (D01 §1.1). What an unregistered opcode does differs per parser
  (W05 §5.1, L01 §4.1).
- **Heartbeat**: a side that has received nothing for 30 s sends 0x0804; the peer answers 0x0805; 180 s more without
  traffic closes the link (same constants in every process: W05 §1.5, L01 §2.6, S02 §4.4, D05 knowledge). The game servers also heartbeat clients (§5.1); Login does
  it even to pre-login clients, which it then closes for answering (L01-B11).
- **Link loss in the WM**: Account lost → the WM shuts down; Character lost → one immediate reconnect, then shutdown;
  AccountLog / GameLog / Login → reconnect (W05 §1.5).
- **Handles in messages**: a server request about a client carries the requester's handle so the answer can be routed:
  Login and WM put the session handle (`u16`) first; zone requests start with the 6-byte `NETPACKETZONEHEADER
  {u16 objHandle, u32 charNo}` that the answer echoes (Z01 knowledge §6, W01 knowledge). The 2017 PDB structs omit
  these prefixes (C02 knowledge §3).

### 2.4 Processing model

| Process | Threads | Order guarantees |
|---|---|---|
| Login | IOCP workers (2 × CPU), acceptor, one main thread that pumps **every** buffered frame of each session per pass (5 ms sleep) | per session in order |
| WM | IOCP workers (2 × CPU), acceptor, main thread: **one** frame per session per pass; `ProcessContents` runs the timed services (W05 §2) | per session in order |
| Zone | IOCP workers (2 × CPU + 1), acceptor, main thread `zs_mainthreadfunction` (handlers, timers, player tick, mob AI; Z09 §1), certification threads (Z10 §6) | per session in order; `g_Now` in tenths of a second (Z09 knowledge) |
| DataServers | a listener / IOCP layer and N session workers, each with its own ODBC connection, taking packets from one global queue (D01 §1, D05 knowledge) | **none across workers**: two requests of one sender can run concurrently and finish out of order (D05 knowledge; D03 knowledge: most multi-step requests are not transactional) |

All handlers of the three game servers run on their main thread, so in-memory state needs no locks there; the
DataServers rely on the database for consistency *(inferred from the thread layout)*.

### 2.5 State ownership and persistence

| State | Owner while running | Stored in | Written | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| accounts, passwords, block and auth flags | Account DB | `Account` (`tUser`, `tUserAuth`) | by procedures on request; Login and WM only read | D01 §3 |
| login / logout history, IP blocks, statistics | AccountLog DB | `AccountLog` (`tAccountLog`, `tIPBlock`), `StatisticsData` | Login at login and world select, WM at logout and for user counts | D01 §4 |
| character rows, shape, options, tutorial | Character DB; between zones the **WM cache** | `w00_Character` (`tCharacter`, …) | read by the WM at character select (0x1004 → 0x1005 + one blob per DB packet, kept in the WM session); the zone saves to the Character server (`ws_save2DB`: 0x4001 … every 600 s and on other triggers) and to the WM cache (`ws_save2worldmanager`, dept 16 `2WLDMAN` blobs, only when the player links to another zone; §3.13); the WM sends the cache to the next zone (0x101C …) and 0x1037 at logout | W01 §6.3, Z01 §8.4, Z09 §2, Z12 §6, D02 §4–§6 |
| items, money, storage | the zone (in memory) with the Character server as the store | `tItem`, `tItemOptions` and side tables | per change through ITEMDB: reserve → DB → apply, or apply → DB with disconnect on failure (Z05 §1); the Character server trusts the zone's keys, lots and prices (D03 knowledge) | Z04, Z05, D03 |
| quests, skills, abstates, titles | the zone | `tQuest`, skill / state tables | CHARSAVE and the dept 17 / 18 / 24 requests | Z15 §5, Z23 §7, D04 §1–§4 |
| guilds, academies, guild storage | the WM (loaded from the Character DB at start, W03 §9.1); zones keep mirrors | guild tables in `w00_Character` | WM ↔ Character round trips on every change (W03 §2) | W03, Z08 §8, §10, D04 §6, §9 |
| friends, prison, options, holy promise | WM / zone per feature | `tFriend` and feature tables | WM or zone requests | W04, Z08 §6, §9, D04 |
| party, raid | the WM; zones keep mirrors | not stored *(inferred: no W02 handler sends a Character-server request)* | – | W02 §1, Z08 §1–§2 |
| map objects, mobs, drops, booths, trades in progress | the zone | not stored | – | Z09, Z06 |
| game log | GameLog DB (write-only) | `w00_GameLog.tGameLog`; NC_SYSLOG hourly text files | zones, WM and Character server send records; only 0x0402 is answered | D05 |

### 2.6 Where a player's session lives

1. **Login** (client link 1): version check, account check through Account and AccountLog, world list, world select
   (Login asks the WM with 0x0C0D and gives the client the WM address and a 32-word validate key).
2. **WM** (client link 2, kept until the client quits or returns to the world list): world login with the key
   (0x0C0F), the character list from the Character server, character select (0x1001): the WM loads the character,
   finds the zone that registered the login map and gives the client that zone's address (0x1003).
3. **Zone** (client link 3, replaced on every zone change): map login 0x1801; the zone takes the ticket from the WM
   (0x104B) and receives the cached character (0x101C … 0x101D) before it answers 0x1802. A map change to another
   zone goes through the WM (0x180D / 0x180E) and a new client connection; a change inside the zone stays local
   (Z01 §7). Returning to the world list uses a one-time token Login issued through the WM (L01 §4.3).

The message-level sequences are §3 (session and persistence §3.2–§3.15; cross-server gameplay from §3.16, part 6).

### 2.7 Deployment limits (from K01)

- One `WORLD_NAME` row and one WM per world; Login indexes worlds by number (L01-B1, L01-B2 for out-of-range numbers).
- Zone numbers 0 … 11, contiguous (the ring follows `zone + 1` and wraps to 0 at the first gap); a map belongs to one
  zone by the `_ServerGroup.txt` column of `World/Field.txt` (or `World/<worldName>/Field.txt`); value 99 = "every zone
  its own copy" (the mini-house `MHBasic0` … `MHBasic3`, single-digit suffix) (K01 §5–§6).
- The shipped rows bind every listener to `0.0.0.0` but the WM compares a zone's address with its row exactly
  (W05 knowledge §3, open question; X02).

## 3. Cross-server sequence index

Feature → messages → handler in each process. §3.2–§3.15 cover a player's session and the persistence paths (part 5);
the cross-server gameplay features follow from §3.16 (part 6); §3.31 (added by X04) holds the one-zone flows a second client saw in the captures, §3.32 (added by X06) the skill, stat-point, abnormal-state and death flows of capture batch 4. Every row names the handler that **receives** the
message; the behaviour inside it is in the cited §, which this index does not repeat. Statements of §5 are already
applied here (for example the seed at accept, the Login 0x0C36 field order, the 600-s save).

### 3.1 Notation

- Processes: **C** client, **L** Login, **A** Account, **AL** AccountLog, **W** WM, **Z** zone (**ZA** / **ZB** the
  old and the new zone of a map change), **D** Character server, **G** GameLog. `X→Y` = X sends, Y receives.
- Handler column: `<receiver> FUN_…` = address in that process's 2016 binary (`re-data/exports/<bin>`); `client
  FUN_…` = the 2016 client.
- Names: official opcode names from the 2016 server PDBs and the 2017 `PROTOCOL_COMMAND_*` enums (one name source
  per value, §1.1); the 2017 name of a value the 2016 code uses differently is not given. *(captured)* marks an order
  or timing seen in `re-data/captures` (`docs/protocol/capture-validation.md` §3–§10 and §16–§25; its §11 checks the orders against this index); the capture-validation § of the observation is given where the Docs column does not name it. X04 added the observations of P02's capture batches 2 and 3 (server links, start-up, saves, combat, character create / delete, items, a second client). X06 added batch 4 (`s37b`, `s38`, `s24`; capture-validation §22–§25): the create and rename refusals, the tutorial skip, a zone down, back to character select and quit on the links, the lost zone connection, zone kills, the auto-save, skills, stat points, reset items, abnormal states, death and revive by skill. Where a batch-4 row of capture-validation §11 confirms a step it is marked *(captured)* with its §; where it corrects one (W01 §5.1 / Z01 §8.1 quit, Z01 §8.3 Kickout, Z01 §8.4 auto-save) the step states the corrected flow (§3.12, §3.13; the old statements are in §5.3 / §5.4).
- Log records (AccountLog, GameLog, NC_SYSLOG) are listed once, in §3.15, not in every flow.

### 3.2 Connection set-up

| Link | Sequence | Handlers | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|
| C → L, W, Z | the server sends `0x0807 NC_MISC_SEED_ACK {u16 seed}` right after accept; from then on client → server bytes are XOR-499 encrypted from that index. The zone takes the seed from a global counter (+1 per connection); the client never sends `0x0806` *(captured)* | L L01 §1.4; W `Client_SendSeed` FUN_00430550; Z `Send_NC_MISC_SEED_ACK` FUN_0045b120 (accept, Z18 §1.3); Z `0x0806` handler FUN_00451a30 (unused) | §2.3, §5.1, Z01 §5.1 |
| server → server | listener `0x0801` → connector `0x0802 {echo, to, from, world, zone, key}` → listener `0x0803 {echo, result}`; then `0x0804` / `0x0805` heartbeats (30 s / +180 s); the three handshake packets pass in one ms per link *(captured, capture-validation §17.2)* | per parser: L01 §4.2, W01 §2.1, W05 §5.2, Z01 §3.3, D01 §2, D05 §5 | §2.3 |
| WM start *(captured)* | the WM opens its links to AccountLog, Account, Character, GameLog and Login within 3 ms, each with the handshake above; Login's link sends `0x0808 NC_MISC_USER_COUNT_CMD` in the handshake ms as `{0, 0}`, so the world stays status 5 "Server is full" until the next `0x0808` 30.0 s later ((original bug) P02-B22); the Character-server start-up load runs in W03 §9.1 order, one request per answer, and ends with the `0x9804 NC_GUILD_ACADEMY_START_DB_GET_RANKING_LIST_ACK` answer when `dAggregationDate` is 0 (CharDB link state 1; no `0x9805` / `0x9806`, W05 §1.3 as corrected in §5.3); at a stop the five links close within 2 ms | W `0x0803` per parser (AccountDB FUN_00407d50, AccountLogDB FUN_00408e00, CharDB FUN_00409c50, GameLogDB FUN_00424160, Login FUN_00424720); `CWMLoginSession::SendUserCount` FUN_00438d10; W `0x9804` FUN_0040ccc0 / FUN_00441aa0 | W05 §1.2, §1.3, W03 §9.1, W01 §2.1, L01 world-status table; capture-validation §17.2, §17.3, §17.5 |
| zone start *(captured)* | per zone process 4 Character, 4 GameLog and 4 WM links; the WM refuses a socket while another one from the same IPv4 address waits in its accepted ring (`CSocket_Acceptor::SetAccepted` FUN_00463c40), so of 4 WM links opened together the 2nd and 4th are dropped and reconnect after the 20.0-s retry (WM and GameDB 20 s, GameLog 600 s): ≈ 30 s on 2 links, ≈ 50 s until 4 ((original bug) P02-B25); on each accepted WM link `0x0801` → `0x0802` → `0x0803` → Z→W `0x180F` (map registration on every link that registers; a new map entry does not store `IsReleaseMap`, (original bug) P02-B26) + `0xA419`, and `0x7446 NC_GUILD_ZONE_LIST_REQ` once per process on the round-robin link → W→Z `0x0821 NC_MISC_TIMEFROMWORLD_CMD`; the first GameDB link's `0x0803` sends Z→D `0xBCD5 NC_GAMBLE_SLOTMACHINE_DB_GAMEINFO_REQ` once per process (`smjpr_RankingReq` FUN_00626220, to the Character server, not the WM); the WM asks the Character server `0xA41A` once per WM start (32 ms after the first `0xA419`); later WM broadcasts reach the zone on its last-registered link | Z `0x0803` FUN_005a3320 (WM), FUN_00468880 (GameDB), FUN_0046a190 (GameLog); W `0x180F` FUN_00428800 → `SetServiceMapZoneNo` FUN_004391d0; W `0xA419` FUN_00428870; Z `0x0821` FUN_004511a0; Z `0xBCD5` FUN_00626220 | Z01 §3.3, Z09 §1.5, W01 §7.1, W02 §1.6, W05 §5.2, S02 §1.4, Z06 §5; capture-validation §17.2, §17.4, §17.5 |
| C → W, C → Z | the first client packet must be `0x0C0F` (WM) or `0x1801` (zone); before `0x1803` the zone dispatches only `0x1803` and `0x0805` | W parser FUN_00419690; Z `ClientSession::zbs_Parsing` FUN_004fb9f0 | W01 §3.2, Z18 §1.3 |

### 3.3 Account login with password *(captured)*

| # | Hop | Message | Handler | What happens | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | C→L | `0x0C65 NC_USER_CLIENT_VERSION_CHECK_REQ {sVersionKey[64]}` | L FUN_004060d0 | key compared with the configured keys → `0x0C67 …RIGHTVERSION_CHECK_ACK` or `0x0C66 …WRONGVERSION_CHECK_ACK` (client quits; L00-B1) | login §4 |
| 2 | C→L | `0x0C5A NC_USER_US_LOGIN_REQ {sUserName[260], sPassword[36], spawnapps}`, sent by the client on 0x0C67 (client FUN_0046f470) | L FUN_00406450 | exact length 0x13E, else dropped; forwarded with the client IP | login §4 |
| 3 | C→L | `0x0C04 NC_USER_XTRAP_REQ`, same client tick | L FUN_00406230 | key not checked (L00-B5); `0x0C05 NC_USER_XTRAP_ACK {1}` at once | login §4 |
| 4 | L→A | `0x0C5B NC_USER_US_PASSWORD_CHECK_REQ` (0x152) | A FUN_00402fe0 | `usp_User_USLoginGame` → `0x0C5C {nError 0x41 ok / 0x43 DB error, nUserNo, nAuthID, nBlock, bLoginAble, …}` | D01 §3.2 |
| 5 | A→L | `0x0C5C NC_USER_US_PASSWORD_CHECK_ACK` | L FUN_00404590 | userNo −1 → 0x45 "Please check ID or Password.", −2 → 0x4B agreement, login closed → 0x45 (L00-B2), blocked → 0x47, not authorised → 0x48 maintenance: `0x0C09 NC_USER_LOGINFAIL_ACK {err}` and disconnect; OK → next step (blocked `{0x47}` / not login-able `{0x48}` *(captured, capture-validation §16.1)*: AL `0x0720` + `0xC802`, no `0x0C5D`) | login §5.1, D01 §3.2 |
| 6 | L→AL | `0x0C5D NC_USER_US_IS_IP_BLOCK_REQ` (0x17B) | AL FUN_004038d0 | `p_IPBlock_IsBlock`: blocked = IP in `tIPBlock` **and** the account never selected a world; DB error = not blocked | D01 §4.3 |
| 7 | AL→L | `0x0C5E NC_USER_US_IS_IP_BLOCK_ACK` | L FUN_004058c0 | blocked → `0x0C09 {0x51}` "You are not in our service area."; else register the session, `0x0C16 NC_USER_KICKOFFFROMWORLD_CMD {userNo}` to **every** WM (§3.12 f; 6 header bytes never written, (original bug) P02-B5) and AL `0xC801` in the same ms *(captured, capture-validation §7.2)*, `0x0C0A NC_USER_LOGIN_ACK` world list (70–170 ms after 0x0C5A *(captured)*) | login §5.2 |
| 8 | C→L | `0x0C1B NC_USER_WORLD_STATUS_REQ`, 10 ms after 0x0C0A, then every 10.0 s while the list is shown *(captured)* | L FUN_004062f0 | `0x0C1C NC_USER_WORLD_STATUS_ACK` (same body as 0x0C0A); a closed world `{0}` *(captured, capture-validation §16.1)*; status 5 for 30 s after a WM start (P02-B22, §3.2) | login §4 |

A wrong password ends at step 5 (`0x0C09 {0x45}` ~50 ms after 0x0C5A *(captured)*); the next attempt is a new TCP
connection with a new seed. The link round trips of steps 4–7 run in this order in every captured login *(captured, capture-validation §7.2)*. Only this path is reachable from the 2016 client; the other login variants of Login and
Account (0x0C08, 0x0C3A, 0x0C3F, 0x0C4C, 0x0C27 …) serve older clients (login §5.1, D01 §3.2–§3.4).

### 3.4 One-time-token login (back from the WM to the world list) *(captured)*

| # | Hop | Message | Handler | What happens | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | C→W | `0x0C33 NC_USER_WILL_WORLD_SELECT_REQ` (character list or in game) | W FUN_00417330 | forwards to Login | W01 §5.1 |
| 2 | W→L | `0x0C35 NC_USER_CREATE_OTP_REQ {u16 handle, u32 userNo, userId[256], u32 ip}` | L FUN_00408a20 | 32-char token (FUN_0040a8a0), valid 10 s; result 0x1E58 ok, 0x1E59 / 0x1E5A failure, 0x1E5B a token is pending | login §5.3, L01 §4.3 |
| 3 | L→W | `0x0C36 NC_USER_CREATE_OTP_ACK {u16 result, u16 wmHandle, char token[32]}` | W FUN_00424a70 | `0x0C34 NC_USER_WILL_WORLD_SELECT_ACK {result, token}` to the client (+20…44 ms *(captured)*) | W01 §5.1 (field order: §5.2 of this file); capture-validation §16.1 |
| 4 | C | client CharSelect FUN_004766a0 | | 0x1E58: keeps the token, leaves the game (§3.12 a) or the character list (§3.12 d), connects to Login | C01 |
| 5 | C→L | `0x0807` seed, `0x0C65` / `0x0C67`, then `0x0C37 NC_USER_LOGIN_WITH_OTP_REQ {sOTP[32]}` and `0x0C04` | L FUN_00406bb0 | exact length 0x22; unknown or expired token → `0x0C09 {0x52}` (no client text, L00-B4); login closed → 0x45 (L00-B2); success: `0x0C16` to every WM, `0x0C0A` **before** the `0x0C05` *(captured)* (no DB round trip), token removed | login §4, L01 §4.3 |

On the links *(captured, capture-validation §16.1)*: `0x0C33` → L `0x0C35` → `0x0C36 {0x1E58, hWM, sOTP}` → C `0x0C34`; the client then closes the WM link (§3.12 d), and the WM sends A `0x0C11`, AL `0x0722` + `0xC803` and every zone `0x0C19` in one ms; at step 5 Login's `0x0C16` (stale header bytes as in §3.3 step 7, P02-B5) reaches the WM, which passes it to every zone.

| 3 | W→L | `0x0C0E NC_USER_WILLLOGIN_ACK {copy, u8 ok = 1}` | L FUN_00408870 | `0x0C0C NC_USER_WORLDSELECT_ACK {status, WM ip and port, validate[32]}` to the client, 20–30 ms after 0x0C0B *(captured)* | login §5.3 |
| 4 | C | client login scene | | closes the Login link, connects to the WM | login §8 |

With the test DB's 2018 procedure the DB kept the old name, so the map login under the new name failed (§3.9 step 3, `0x689`): an effect of the test DB, not a server bug | W01 §4.1, D02 §3.5; capture-validation §22.3 |

### 3.8 Character select: load and zone address *(captured)*

| # | Hop | Message | Handler | What happens | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | C→W | `0x1001 NC_CHAR_LOGIN_REQ {u8 slot}` | W FUN_00422be0 | slot ≥ 6 → `0x1002 NC_CHAR_LOGINFAIL_ACK {0x143}`, empty → 0x144; unfinished tutorial: step 0 → `0x1110 NC_CHAR_TUTORIAL_POPUP_REQ` and stop (step 1b), else map "RouT00" + `0x111D`; instance-map redirect FUN_00452580; no zone has the map, or the previous character's logout is not yet confirmed (`playingCharNo`, cleared only by the zone's 0x0C31, §3.12) → 0x145 "The Map is under maintenance"; else D `0x1004`. *(captured, capture-validation §22.1, §23.6)*: a map whose zone is down → `0x1002 {0x145}` in 2–12 ms without a CharDB round trip (the WM tells nobody else of a dead zone); the client stays on the list, and the first `0x1001` after the zone's `0x0803` / `0x180F` succeeds | W01 §4.2, §5.4; capture-validation §22.1, §23.6 |

### 3.11 Map change

**Inside one zone** (destination map on the same zone, or `_ServerGroup` 99): `so_LinkTo` FUN_0049ad30 sends W
`0x180B NC_MAP_LINKSTART_CMD` (W FUN_00429c90: `linking = 1`), unmarks the player (`so_Unmark` FUN_00558900,
`REMOVE_NOW` → character save, §3.13), moves it and sends C `0x1809 NC_MAP_LINKSAME_CMD {mapIndex, x, y}` (client
FUN_0049feb0; the revive-at-another-map path sends `0x104F` instead). The client reloads the map and sends `0x1803`
again → §3.10 (another `0x180C`). Z01 §7.1. The revive branch is captured (§3.13, capture-validation §9.5).

**To another zone** *(captured: gate, town portal)*:

| # | Hop | Message | Handler | What happens | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | ZA | `so_LinkTo` FUN_0049ad30 | | an open trade is cancelled first (or the link dropped), locked items refuse it ("Link during item operation"); W `0x180B`; full DB save `ws_save2DB(p, 0, 2)` and WM cache save `ws_save2worldmanager` FUN_004536f0 (§3.13); player mode 0; W `0x180D`; G `0xC809`. *(captured)*: W `0x180B`, the D save + G `0x0410 {StoreType 2}`, the WM burst `0x400E` … `0x4037` round robin over the 4 WM links in 1–2 ms (P02-B12; on one link the tail leaves 5–16 ms late, Nagle), `0x180D`, `0xC809`; the hunt log is lost ((original bug) P02-B20, §3.15) | Z01 §7.1; capture-validation §7.5.4, §10.1, §10.2 |
| 2 | ZA→W | `0x180D NC_MAP_LINKRESERVE_REQ {objHandle, charNo, wmHandle, destZone, destMap, x, y, mapIndex, x, y, zoneIp[16], zonePort, wmHandle}` | W FUN_0042f420 | map-login ticket = 1, pending link with the destination; `0x180E` back to ZA on the link the `0x180D` came on *(captured)* | W01 §7.3 |
| 3 | W→ZA | `0x180E NC_MAP_LINKRESERVE_ACK {ref, mapIndex, x, y, zoneIp, zonePort, wmHandle}` | Z FUN_0049b510 | HP 0 or mode 3 → only the log "Link Fail Player Die": no packet to the client, nothing undone (the player stays in ZA in mode 0 with `sp_MiscFlag.JustLink` set, the WM keeps ticket and pending link; (original bug) X01-B5, §6.2.1); else C `0x180A NC_MAP_LINKOTHER_CMD {mapIndex, x, y, zoneIp, zonePort, wmHandle}` (10–16 ms after `0x180E` *(captured)*) and `so_banning(p, 0)` (removed; its save is skipped because the mode is 0) | Z01 §7.1, §8.3 |
| 4 | C→ZB | client GameFrameWork FUN_004b4ab0 connects to ZB; `0x080D` to the WM in the same tick *(captured)* | | §3.9 with the reserved destination in the base blob (step 4), then §3.10; the WM repeats only `0x5826` and `0x182A` *(captured)* | capture-validation §4.1 |

Wing, town portal and multi-link NPCs (Z01 §7.2–§7.3), revive (Z07 §3) and silver wing (Z26) end in `so_LinkTo`.

### 3.12 Leaving the game, kicks and duplicate logins

**a) In game → world list** *(captured, idle_in_world)*:

| # | Hop | Message | Handler | What happens | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | C→Z | `0x2012 NC_ACT_STOP_REQ`, `0x1071 NC_CHAR_LOGOUTREADY_CMD` | Z FUN_00558450 | logout allowed after 8 s; action timer +13 s → `sp_Schedule_Logout` | Z07 §3.3 |
| 1b | C→W | `0x7011`, `0x7015`, `0x7016`, `0x703B` (same tick) | W04 §8.2 | client options saved through the WM to the Character server, no answers | W04 §8.2 |
| 2 | C→W→L | after the client's 10-s countdown: `0x0C33` → token | | §3.4 steps 1–3 | |
| 3 | C→W, C→Z | `0x0C18 NC_USER_NORMALLOGOUT_CMD {0}` to both, same tick | | | |
| 4 | Z | `0x0C18` | Z FUN_00558690 → `sp_Schedule_Logout` FUN_00558160 | reads no `LogoutType`; once the `0x1071` time + 8 s (`p+0x2a798`) has passed: unsummon, `so_Unmark(REMOVE_NOW, …, 9)` → full save to D (dept 16) + G `0x0410 {StoreType 9}`; W `0x0C31` in the same ms; object freed, client link closed *(captured, capture-validation §22.5, §23.2, §25.9)*. This holds when the zone handles this `0x0C18` before the WM's `0x0C19` (step 8); otherwise see (b) | Z01 §8.1 (as corrected in §5.3) |
| 5 | W | `0x0C18 {0}` | W FUN_00412df0 | zones `0x0C19`; close reason 0x780; `CharacterLogout` FUN_00436d50 (party / raid / guild / friends / instance / chat leave → W02–W04, D `0x1037`, AL `0x081F`, G `0xC807`, AL `0x0843` after ≥ 60 min); state 3 → `Client_OnClosed` FUN_00437030: A `0x0C11 NC_USER_LOGOUT_DB` (no-op), AL `0x0722`, `0xC803`, zones `0x0C19` again (W05-B25, Extended: each `0x0C19` that finds the player still in the zone saves again, (b)). *(captured, capture-validation §22.5)*: the first `0x0C19` with `0x1037` / `0x081F` / `0xC807` 1 ms after the `0x0C18`, the second with `0x0C11 {playmin, 0x780}` / `0x0722` / `0xC803` 17 ms later | W01 §5.1, §5.4, W05 §4.5 |
| 6 | W→D | `0x1037 NC_CHAR_LOGOUT_DB {charNo, map, x, y, playmin, state, Cen}` | D FUN_00405b30 | `p_Char_Logout` adds the play minutes; G `0x040B`. Map, position and money come from the WM cache (the character login's blob, updated by a link to another map / zone but not by same-map links) and are only logged, so G `0x040B` records stale money and position ((original bug) P02-B36) *(captured, capture-validation §22.5, §23.5, §25.9)* | D02 §5 |
| 7 | Z→W | `0x0C31 NC_USER_POSSIBLE_NEW_CONNECT_CMD {…, wmHandle, charNo}` | W FUN_0042c1c0 | `playingCharNo = −1` (a later `0x1001` is allowed); logs an error if the session is gone | W01 §7.3 |
| 8 | W→Z | `0x0C19 NC_USER_CONNECTCUT2ZONE_CMD {ref = 0xFF…, userNo}` | Z FUN_005a6040 → `wms_kickoff` FUN_005a3860 | when the zone handled step 4 first, the player is gone: nothing; otherwise `so_banning` saves with StoreType 4, see (b) *(captured, capture-validation §25.9)* | Z01 §8.3 |
| 9 | C→L | token login | | §3.4 step 5 | |

**b) Quit** *(captured, capture-validation §22.5, §23.3, §25.9)*: as (a) without the token steps: `0x1071`, the client's
10-s countdown, then `0x0C18 {0}` to the zone and the WM in one ms; the WM runs step 5. The zone ends in one of two
ways, and arrival order does not decide which (the client's `0x0C18` was on the wire before the WM's first `0x0C19` in
all three captured quits; why the zone handles the kick first is not traced): **(1)** it handles the client's
`0x0C18` first (`s24` A): step 4 at once, one save (StoreType 9 + `0x0C31`, 7 ms after the request), and the WM's
`0x0C19` find no object; **(2)** it handles the WM's `0x0C19` first (`s37b`, `s24` B): `so_banning` FUN_00558b70 saves
with StoreType 4, `zs_disconnect` FUN_005abb60 closes the state-3 session with 7, the second `0x0C19` saves 4 again
(within 35 ms, each a full burst; three `0x750C` at level 120), and the object ends with StoreType 9 +
`0x0C31` when `0x1071`'s `sp_Schedule_Logout` fires 13 s after `0x1071` (≈ 3 s after the quit): four saves
((original bug) P02-B35; W05-B25 Extended). The Kickout timer of (e) is not armed, because the `0x1071` work is already queued.

**c) Back to character select** (`0x0C18 {1}`, W01 §5.1) *(captured, capture-validation §22.5, §23.2)*: the WM runs
`CharacterLogout` (`0x1037`, `0x081F`, `0xC807` in one ms; the Character server's G `0x040B` 4 ms later) but keeps the
session (`linking = 1`, zone handle 0xFFFF; no `0x0C11`). The client sends the zone the same `0x0C18`, ≥ 8 s after
`0x1071`, so the zone runs (a) step 4 at once (StoreType 9 + `0x0C31` in one ms, 3–10 ms after the `0x0C18`), and the
client closes its zone socket 1.0 s later. It then sends `0x0C1F` with one stray byte (client FUN_00497140; the WM
ignores the payload) → W→D `0x0C12` → `0x0C5F`, `0x0C13` → C `0x0C14` (§3.6 steps 4–5). The option saves of step 1b
reach the Character server as `0x7026` / `0x702A` / `0x702B` / `0x704F` 20–70 ms later, and `0x7050` returns a stale
`nCharNo` ((original bug) D04-B13).

**d) Character
list → world list** *(captured)*: `0x0C33` → `0x0C34`, then the client closes the WM link without `0x0C18`; the WM's
`ProcessDisconnect` runs `Client_OnClosed` (no character logout, A `0x0C11`, AL `0x0722` / `0xC803`, zones `0x0C19`).

**e) Lost connections**: WM: socket error, or 30 + 180 s silence → state 3 → `Client_OnClosed` as (a) step 5 (W05 §4.5;
reason stale, W05-B9). Zone: `zs_disconnect` FUN_005abb60 on state 3 ("Socket cut"): `sp_RefreshWMSSocket` (Z01 §8.2,
W `0x0C31`), `so_Unmark(vt 0x8ec(), 1, 7)` (saves unless that returns `REMOVE_WARP`) and free (Z09 §1.5). The two sides do not tell each other except through
`0x0C19` (WM → zones) and `0x0C31` (zone → WM).

*Captured* (capture-validation §23.3, §23.4, §23.7): `zs_disconnect` saves with StoreType 7; its `sp_RefreshWMSSocket`
call sent no `0x0C31` in any capture (which condition failed is not traced). A **lost zone connection** ends the
client's session: 14–27 ms after the RST the client sends `0x0C18 {0}` to the WM (in-game scene tick client
FUN_004cbf30 → `NetMgr::SendUserLogOutMsg2AllServer` FUN_006d34e0 *(pdb2017 name)*: `0x0C18 {0}` on every open socket,
a 1000-ms busy wait, all sockets closed) and shows "Disconnected from the server."; the WM closes as for a quit
(step 5, `0x0C11 {0x780}`). The zone object, with no `0x1071` work queued, saves 4 (first `0x0C19`), 7, 4 (second
`0x0C19`) and ends 10 s after the first save in `sp_Schedule_Kickout` FUN_00558080: StoreType 3, **no** `0x0C31` (the
Kickout timer armed by `so_Unmark(REMOVE_LOGOUT)`; Z01 §8.3 as corrected in §5.4). Every further kick of the user
inside that window (a new login's `0x0C16`) saves 4 again (P02-B35). A **client killed** during the exit countdown:
the WM's `Client_OnClosed` sends one `0x0C19` broadcast (no `0x0C18`), the zone saves 4 and 7, and the `0x1071`
timer ends the object with 9 + `0x0C31`; the relogin 7.9 s after the kill works.

**Zone process killed** *(captured, capture-validation §23.5, §23.6)*: nothing is saved at the kill. Money changed
before it is already committed (`&getmoney` / pick-up: D `0x3438` → `0x3439` in 15–22 ms, Z11 §7.4), so the next login
has that money, but the position and experience of the last `ws_save2DB` (StoreType 9 or 10). Its players' clients
take the lost-connection path above. The WM sends nothing to the Login, the clients or the other zones; its
broadcasts go to the remaining zone links, `0x1001` for the dead zone's map gets `0x1002 {0x145}` (§3.8 step 1), and
the restarted zone registers as in §3.2 (P02-B25 again).

**f) Kicks and duplicate logins**:

| Trigger | Sequence (receiver handler) | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| the account logs in to Login again (§3.3 step 7, §3.4 step 5) *(captured)* | L→every W `0x0C16 NC_USER_KICKOFFFROMWORLD_CMD {userNo}` (W FUN_00424880 → `Kickoff` FUN_00437d50: C `0x0C17 {0x683}` "multiple logins", close 0x785) → every Z `0x0C16` (Z FUN_005a5f90 → `wms_kickoff` → `so_banning(p, 1)`: `REMOVE_LOGOUT`, save with StoreType 4, `sp_Schedule_Kickout` FUN_00558080 after 10 s: StoreType 3, no `0x0C31`; a kick while the object waits in a logout window saves 4 again, P02-B35 *(captured, capture-validation §23.3, §23.6)*). The WM's 0x0C17 left 0.8 ms before Login's 0x0C0A *(captured)* | W01 §5.2, Z01 §8.3, capture-validation §4.1, §23.3 |
| a second `0x0C0F` of the account at the WM | `Clients_KickDoubleLogin` FUN_00437dc0 (`0x0C17 {0x683}`) and zones `0x0C16`, as §3.6 step 1 | W01 §3.2 |
| the zone throws a player out (`so_Disconnect` FUN_0054f340: SHN checksum, bad ticket, item DB failure 0x684, lot check 0x68C, GM kick …) | Z→W `0x0C1A NC_USER_CONNECTCUT2WORLDMANAGER_CMD {userNo, wmHandle, reason}` (W FUN_00428a40: C `0x0C17 {reason}`); `so_banning` FUN_00558b70 (save, free) | Z01 §8.3, W01 §5.2 |
| GM / OPTool kicks, prison | OPTool kick at the WM and through the ring: §3.28; prison: §3.27 | W04 §4.3, §9, Z01 §10 |

### 3.13 Character persistence

Load: always from the Character server at character select (§3.8: `0x1004` → blocks → `0x1005`). The WM keeps the
blocks as its cache and replays them to every zone the character enters (§3.9 step 4); the zone never reads the
character from the Character server. The cache is dropped when the WM session closes; returning to character select
keeps it until the next `0x1001` reloads.

Saves (`WholeSaver` at DAT_00aa46b0; `ws_save2DB` FUN_00455a10 returns at once when the player's mode is 0;
`RemoveWhen` *(pdb2016)*: `REMOVE_NOW` 0, `REMOVE_LOGOUT` 1, `REMOVE_WARP` 2, `REMOVE_REVIVE` 3; `ShinePlayer::so_Unmark`
FUN_00558900 saves for every value except `REMOVE_WARP`):

| Trigger | Zone path | To the Character server | To the WM cache | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| every 600 s in game (`tAutoSaveTime` +0x2aa40 = now + 6000 ticks of 0.1 s, first set at map-login completion by `so_SetAutoSaveTime` FUN_005608c0 (vt 0xac0, from FUN_00443390), reset by every save; StoreType 10 599.94–599.99 s after the `0x1803` *(captured, capture-validation §23.5)*) | `so_Routine` FUN_0056c790 → `ws_save2DB(p, 1, 10)` | `0x4018` PK count, `0x4001 NC_CHARSAVE_ALL_REQ` (level / exp / fame, position, stones / HP / SP), `0x4007` abstates, `0x4009` skills if changed, `0x400B` titles, `0x401A` rest-exp time, item cool times `0x4023`, pet / minimon saves; **no** `0x4005` quests (second argument 1). *(captured, capture-validation §10.1)*: `0x4018`, `0x4001`, `0x4007`, `0x400B` (only when titles are marked), G `0x0410`, `0x401A`, `0x4035` in one ms; the u16 after the ref is `bufferlocation`, always 0 ((original bug) P02-B21); `0x4008` is answered before `0x4002` | – | Z09 §2, Z01 §8.4 (as corrected in §5.4), D02 §6 |
| level up, `&levelup` | `sp_GainExp` FUN_0042d830, FUN_0042fa00 → `ws_save2DB(p, 1, 8)` | as above | `0x1073 NC_CHAR_EXP_CHANGED_CMD` on every exp gain, `0x1074 NC_CHAR_LEVEL_CHANGED_CMD` (W FUN_0042e160 → G `0xC808`), `0x400E` alone (`ws_savelevel2worldmamager` FUN_00453580). *(captured)*: per level W `0x400E`, `0x1074`, G `0x041E`, the D save, D `0x1070 NC_CHAR_FAMESAVE_CMD` in one ms; the WM applies them in link-service order (`0xC808` logged levels 7, 6, 8; (original bug) P02-B12); then C→Z `0x4025` → D `0x4027` → `0x4028` → C `0x4026` | Z03 §8.3, Z12 §7, W01 §7.5; capture-validation §9.4, §10.6 |
| link to another zone | `so_LinkTo` FUN_0049ad30 → `ws_save2DB(p, 0, 2)` + `ws_save2worldmanager` FUN_004536f0 | as above **plus** `0x4005` quests. `0x4009` holds only the skills cast since the last save, and a skill once more with cool-down 0 after its cool-down ended (Z23 §7.1): D `0x4009` (D FUN_00406760) → `0x400A` (Z FUN_00455570) 8 ms later *(captured, capture-validation §24.7)* | the dept-16 set in code order: `0x400E NC_CHARSAVE_2WLDMAN_ALL_CMD` (when a field is set), `0x400F`, `0x4010` (always), `0x4029`, `0x402B`, `0x4011`, `0x4012` (skills ≠ 0), `0x4013` ×4 items (a failure disconnects with 0x68C and skips the rest), `0x4015`, `0x4014`, `0x4016`, `0x4017`, `0x4021`, `0x402A`, `0xBC1D`, `0x402C`, [`0xCC0B` riding], `0x4032`, [`0x4033` pet state], [`0x4034` cool times], `0x4036`, [`0x4037` sold items] (W FUN_00429d40 … FUN_0042d080, W05 §5.5); one burst over the 4 WM links in 1–2 ms *(captured; Z01 §8.4's list is superseded, §5.4)* | Z01 §7.1, §8.4, W01 §7.4, Z23 §7.1; capture-validation §7.5.4, §24.7 |
| link inside the zone, revive at another map | `so_Unmark` `REMOVE_NOW` / `REMOVE_REVIVE` → `ws_save2DB(p, 0, …)`; revive *(captured)*: `0x104E` (Z FUN_00452980) → W `0x180B`, G `0x042D` (hunt-log flush before the `REMOVE_REVIVE` case), `ws_save2DB(p, 0, 2)`, C `0x1C18` / `0x242A`, `0x104F`; the client's `0x1803` 158 ms later brings the full login burst and W `0x180C`; no WM saves, no `0xC809` | full | – | Z01 §7.1, Z07 §3.1; capture-validation §9.5 |
| logout (the client's `0x0C18` once the `0x1071` time + 8 s has passed, or the `0x1071` timer at +13 s) | `sp_Schedule_Logout` → `so_Unmark(REMOVE_NOW, …, 9)` | full; W `0x0C31` in the same ms *(captured, capture-validation §22.5, §23.3)* | – (the cache is dropped) | Z01 §8.1, §3.12 b |
| WM kick (`0x0C16` / `0x0C19`) | `so_banning(p, 1)` FUN_00558b70 → `so_Unmark(REMOVE_LOGOUT, …, 4)` | full (StoreType 4); then, unless `0x1071`'s logout work is already queued (`TSCW_LOGOUT`), `sp_Schedule_Kickout` FUN_00558080 after 10 s: full again with StoreType 3, object freed, **no** `0x0C31`; every further kick inside the window saves 4 again ((original bug) P02-B35) *(captured, capture-validation §23.3)* | – | Z01 §8.3 (as corrected in §5.4) |
| zone disconnect, link out | `so_banning(p, 0)` → `so_Unmark(REMOVE_NOW, …, 4)` | full (skipped after a link: mode 0) | – | Z01 §8.3 |
| socket lost | `zs_disconnect` FUN_005abb60 → `so_Unmark(vt 0x8ec(), 1, 7)` | full (StoreType 7; also when `so_banning` has put the session in state 3) *(captured, capture-validation §23.3)* | – | Z09 §1.5 |
| zone process killed | none | nothing; the next login loads the last save (position, experience) and every item / money change its own round trip committed *(captured, capture-validation §23.5)* | – | Z11 §7.4, §3.12 e |
| full save, zone change: chat-spam block, buy-back list | full save → `so_SpamSendDataToCharDB` FUN_00562990 (vt 0xab8; only when a block or probation is running, or once to clear it); `ws_save2worldmanager` → `sp_SpamSendDataToWM` FUN_00562bc0 (always) and `sp_SendSellItemInfo_World` (Z31-B6) | `0x4022 NC_CHARSAVE_SET_CHAT_BLOCK_SPAMER_DB_CMD {CharNo, BlockTime, DelayTime}` (D FUN_00423420, `usp_SpamBlock_setTime`) | `0x4021 NC_CHARSAVE_SET_CHAT_BLOCK_SPAMER_WM_CMD` (W FUN_0042c2a0) → next map login W→Z `0x10CB` (Z FUN_0044c9b0 → `so_SpamRecvDataFromWM` FUN_0044a9b0); `0x4037 NC_CHARSAVE_SELL_ITEM_INFO_CMD` (W FUN_0042d080) → W→Z `0x1130` (Z FUN_005a5e80) | Z31 §2.5, §4.2, Z02 §3.4 |
| single values | the owning features | their own requests (quests dept 17, skills 18 (empower `0x4812`, §3.32), options, free stats 0x10D1 (§3.32), …) | `0x1058` class, `0x10C1` option byte, `0x083D` (W01 §7.4) | D02 §6–§7, D04 |

StoreType (the field of G `0x0410`), every value captured (capture-validation §23.3): 2 link / revive, 3 Kickout, 4
`so_banning`, 7 `zs_disconnect`, 8 level-up, 9 logout, 10 auto-save.

`ws_save2worldmanager` has exactly one caller (`so_LinkTo`, other zone), so the WM cache holds the state of the last
zone change plus the single updates *(inferred: capture-validation §25.9 sees the copy refreshed by a link to another
map / zone; the WM handler that refreshes it is not traced)*; `0x1037` at logout carries those values, which
`p_Char_Logout` ignores and the Character server only copies into GameLog `0x040B` ((original bug) P02-B36: stale
money and position; §5.3 row "W01 §5.4 (`CharacterLogout` → CharDB `0x1037`)", §5.7 row "D02 §4"). Of these
requests only `0x4001`, `0x4005`, `0x4007`, `0x4009` and `0x4027` are answered, each only on success (D02 §6); the zone ignores the answers except `0x4002` (level ≥ 60 → W `0x750C`, academy) and `0x4028` (Z01 §8.4).

Ordering: the zone's logout save goes to the Character server on the zone's link, and the next `0x1004` on the WM's
link once the zone's `0x0C31` arrived. The Character server's workers do not order requests of different links
(§2.4), so a re-select that overtakes the save would load the pre-logout rows (X01-B3, *inferred*; the window is the
time a player needs to re-select, so it is rarely open). After a duplicate login (f) the new WM session has
`playingCharNo = −1`, so nothing waits for the old zone's kick save.

### 3.14 Item persistence

Every item change is its own round trip zone → Character server (ITEMDB, dept 13); the zone never sends an item
state to the WM except the item block of a zone change (`0x4013` → `0x102E` to the next zone).

| Step | What happens | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| request | the zone handler checks the request, mints any new item key itself (`ItemKey_Make` FUN_00640710) and sends `NC_ITEMDB_*_REQ {ref, lockindex, …}` | Z04 §1.4, Z05 §1.7 (request → answer table) |
| style 1: reserve → DB → apply | the cells are locked under a lock index, memory unchanged; success → `icl_Apply_N_Free` applies and sends the client its `NC_ITEM_CELLCHANGE_CMD` / … packets, failure → `icl_Free` | Z05 §1.1, §1.3 |
| style 2: apply → DB | drop, pick-up, pick money, `INC_DEC_MONEY`: memory and client change at once, DB-pending flag blocks other item requests; failure → `so_Disconnect(0x684)` (the next login reloads the DB state) | Z05 §1.1 |
| Character server | one handler per request, most multi-step requests not transactional; trusts the zone's keys, lots and prices | D03 §1, D03 knowledge |
| consistency | stack operations compare the zone's lots with the DB's result lots (mismatch → disconnect 0x68C) | Z05 §1.1, §4 |
| zone change | `so_LinkTo` refuses while items are locked; the WM's item block is refreshed by `0x4013` and replayed as `0x102E` ×6 | Z01 §7.1, W01 §6.3 |
| map login | `0x34CF` compares the map-link items with the map (D03 §6.5) | Z01 §6 |

Captured item flows *(captured; "one ms" = sent in the same millisecond; every style-1 request takes the next lock index 0, 1, 2 … of the player, and its answer brings the GameLog record and, in one ms, the client's cell changes and ACK, capture-validation §7.6.1)*:

| Operation | Sequence (receiver handler) | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| split | C→Z `0x300D` (Z FUN_00539a40) → D `0x3434 NC_ITEMDB_SPLIT_REQ` (D FUN_004104b0; 68 bytes, `lotcreate.attr` never written, (original bug) P02-B30) 10–17 ms later → `0x3435 NC_ITEMDB_SPLITSUC_ACK` (Z FUN_00539740) → G `0x0457`, C `0x3001` ×2, `0x300E {0x441}` in one ms; an occupied target cell → `0x300E {0x446}`, an invalid cell `0x445`, without a DB request | Z04 §4.2, Z05 §4, D03 §3.3; capture-validation §19.3 |
| merge | C→Z `0x300B` (Z FUN_00537170) → D `0x342E` (D FUN_0040bbb0) → `0x342F` (Z FUN_00533630) → G `0x0456`, C `0x3001` ×2, `0x300C {0x241}` | Z04 §4.1, Z05 §4, D03 §3.3; capture-validation §19.3 |
| drop | C→Z `0x3007 NC_ITEM_DROP_REQ` (Z FUN_00529f50) → menu `0x3C01` + `0x3008 {0x301}` → `0x3C02` → D `0x3416` (D FUN_0040b140) and C `0x3001` in one ms (style 2) → `0x3417` (Z FUN_0052ab80) → G `0x0447`, `0x1C0A` to the others, then to the dropper with the second `0x3008 {0x301}`; part of a stack → `0x3008 {0x303}` before the menu (Z04-B4, marked superseded: `ii_DropLot` stays reachable when the stack grows while the confirmation is open, not captured; §5.9) | Z04 §3.1, Z05 §3; capture-validation §19.4 |
| pick-up | onto a stack: D `0x341F` (D FUN_0040b980), C `0x3001` and `0x3014` to the others in one ms → `0x3420` (Z FUN_00530310) → C `0x300A`; into a new cell: D `0x341C` (D FUN_0040b760) → `0x341D` (Z FUN_00530110) → `0x300A`; owner-only time = `looting` in ms (20000 → 19.9–19.95 s; Z05 §3.1's unit is superseded, §5.6), then `0x1C0A attr 8` again | Z04 §3.3, Z05 §3.1, Z09 §11.4; capture-validation §19.4 |
| use | C→Z `0x3015` (Z FUN_005416b0) → after the cast (34 ms) D `0x343E` USELOT (D FUN_0040d5e0) / `0x343F` USEALL and C `0x301A` in one ms → `0x3440` (Z FUN_0053fa40) → G `0x0459` / `0x045A`, C `0x3016 {0x700}`, `0x3001`, `0x240E` together; full HP → `0x3016 {0x713}` without a DB request | Z04 §6.3, Z13 §8, Z05 §6.1, D03 §4; capture-validation §19.6 |
| skill book | bought as any item (`0x3003` → D `0x340A` → … `0x3004 {0x201}`; the skill master's `0x3C0A` lists the books of every class); use C→Z `0x3015` (Z FUN_005416b0) → D `0x343F` USEALL (D FUN_0040d800) and C `0x301A` at once → `0x3440 {0x700}` (Z FUN_0053fa40; 14 ms) → `UseItemSkillScroll` → D `0x4801` (D FUN_00411f10) → `0x4802` (Z FUN_0044ec80; 11 ms) → G `0x0432` (map "Need", x / y 0, D03-B11) → C `0x3016 {0x700}`, `0x3001`, `0x4804`, `0x1035` in one burst | Z05 §6.1, Z23 §6.2, Z03 §9.3, D04 §2; capture-validation §24.1, §24.2 |
| reset capsules (stats, empower) | C→Z `0x3015` → `sp_StatEmpowInitialize` FUN_005183f0 → D `0x9005` (D FUN_00416e60) / `0x9006` (D FUN_00417160) and C `0x3016 {0x700}` in one ms, before the DB → `0x9007` / `0x9008` (Z FUN_00518650 / FUN_00518780; 20–28 ms) → G `0x045A` → C `0x3001` (cell emptied) → `0x900C {130}` (every stat point) / `0x900D {49}` (every empower point), no text; the stat reset adds `0x1035` + `0x240E` (MaxHP falls by the free bonus, HP clamped) | Z08 §11, Z23 §4, D03 §9; capture-validation §24.5 |
| storage | open: 4 × D `0x3444` (D FUN_0040da60; one per Character link, in one ms, 3 ms after `0x201D`) → each `0x3446` (Z FUN_0044c460) → its `0x3C08` as it arrives (no `0x3028`); deposit C→Z `0x301C` (Z FUN_00533dc0) → D `0x345F` (D FUN_00410fa0) → G `0x045E` and `0x3460` (Z FUN_00534010) in one ms → C `0x1033`, `0x301D {balance after}`; withdraw `0x301E` (Z FUN_005342b0) → D `0x3461` (D FUN_00411200) → G `0x045F` and `0x3462` (Z FUN_00534500) → C `0x1033`, `0x301F` | Z04 §7.1, §7.2, Z05 §7, D03 §5.1; capture-validation §20.1 |
| sell | C→Z `0x3006 NC_ITEM_SELL_REQ` (Z FUN_00539020) → D `0x3425` SELLLOT (D FUN_00409ec0) / `0x3422` SELLALL (D FUN_00409a20) → `0x3426` / `0x3423` (Z FUN_00539510 / FUN_005393e0) with G `0x0449` + `0xC80E` in one ms → C `0x1033`, `0x3001`, `0x305C`, `0x3005 {0x381}` | Z04 §2.4, Z05 §2.2, D03 §2.2; capture-validation §20.2 |
| buy back | C→Z `0x3059 NC_ITEM_REPURCHASE_REQ` (Z FUN_00538580) → D `0x34BB` (D FUN_0042e380) → G `0x07FD`, `0x34BC` (Z FUN_00538430) and `0xC815` in one ms → C `0x1033`, `0x3001` (first cell of the item with room), `0x305D`, `0x305A {0x3668}`; another NPC's role current → `0x305A {0x3671}` (silent) without a DB request ((original bug) P02-B31) | Z04 §2.5, Z05 §2.2; capture-validation §20.2 |

### 3.15 Log records along the session

| Event | Record (sender → receiver handler) | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| failed account login | L→AL `0x0720 NC_LOG_USER_LOGINFAIL` (AL FUN_00402470, `tAccountLog` type 0; `sUserPassword` = the first 16 characters of the client's MD5 hex string, not the typed password *(captured, capture-validation §16.3)*, §5.7), `0xC802 NC_SYSLOG_ACCOUNT_LOGIN_FAILURE` (AL FUN_004033d0) *(captured: blocked and not login-able accounts, capture-validation §16.1)* | D01 §4.1, §4.5 |
| successful account login | L→AL `0xC801 NC_SYSLOG_ACCOUNT_LOGIN_SUCCESS` (AL FUN_00403310; `UserIP` = the client's dotted IP *(captured, capture-validation §7.3)*, the former L00-B6 is superseded, §5.2, §5.9); in the ms of Login's `0x0C16` to the WM *(captured, §7.2)* | D01 §4.5 |
| world select | L→AL `0x0721 NC_LOG_USER_LOGIN` (AL FUN_00402580, type 1; also ends the IP-block exemption of §3.3 step 6) | D01 §4.1 |
| character created | W→G `0x0411 NC_LOG_GAME_CREATE_AVATAR` (G FUN_00403560), `0xC804` (G FUN_00406e60) *(captured, capture-validation §16.1)* | D05 §2.3, §6.2 |
| character deleted | W→G and D→G `0x0412 NC_LOG_GAME_DELETE_AVATAR` (G FUN_00403560, two rows: the Character server's in the ms of its `0x140B`, the WM's with `0xC805`), W→G `0xC805` (G FUN_00406ee0) *(captured, capture-validation §16.1)* | D05 §2.3 |
| character enters the game | W→G `0xC806` (G FUN_00406f60); D→G `0x040A NC_LOG_GAME_LOGIN` (G FUN_004032b0, also `usp_Login_setChannel`; 1 ms after the WM's `0xC806` *(captured, capture-validation §7.4)*) | D05 §2.2, §6.2 |
| map login done | W→G `0x040F NC_LOG_GAME_LINK` (G FUN_00403490; 0–6 ms after the zone's `0x10B3` *(captured, capture-validation §10.2)*) | D05 §2.3 |
| zone change | Z→G `0xC809 NC_SYSLOG_CHAR_ZONE_TRANSITION` (G FUN_004070e0; after the WM burst, before the WM's `0x180E` *(captured, capture-validation §7.5.4)*) | D05 §6.2 |
| every character save | Z→G `0x0410 NC_LOG_GMAE_LOGOUT_ZONEINFO` (G FUN_00403490; StoreType = save reason; `StoreType 2` at a link, with the save burst *(captured, capture-validation §10.1)*; every value 2 / 3 / 4 / 7 / 8 / 9 / 10 captured, list in §3.13 *(captured, capture-validation §23.3)*) | D05 §2.3, Z01 §8 |
| level up | W→G `0xC808` (G FUN_00407060), Z→G `0x041E` in the level's ms; the WM logs the levels in link-service order (P02-B12) *(captured, capture-validation §9.4)* | D05 §6.2 |
| monster kill | Z→G `0xC80B` and W `0x1073` in the ms of the killing hit *(captured, capture-validation §9.3)* | Z03 §3.2, §8.1, D05 §6.2 |
| player death | Z→G `0x042B` + `0xC80A` in the mob tick that kills *(captured, capture-validation §9.5)* | Z03 §7.2, D05 |
| unmark (logout, link, …) | Z→G `0x042D` hunt log (`mhl_SendAll2Log`, Z09 §28.12), only when the player's mode ≠ 0: a link to another zone sets mode 0 before the unmark and loses the entries ((original bug) P02-B20); same-zone links, revive and logout flush them, a kill's entry waits for the next of these *(captured, capture-validation §9.3, §9.5)* | Z03 §8.2 |
| skill learned (skill book) | D→G `0x0432 NC_LOG_GAME_SKILL_LEARN` after the Character server's `0x4802` (map "Need", x / y 0: (original bug) D03-B11) *(captured, capture-validation §24.2)*; flow §3.14 | D04 §2, D03 |
| item used up (potions, stat-down scroll, capsules) | D→G `0x0459 NC_LOG_GAME_ITEM_USELOT` (lot after) / `0x045A NC_LOG_GAME_ITEM_USEALL`, logged by the Character server before the zone's `0x3001`; the Stat Reset Scroll's `0x0459` follows `0x10D2`, the reset capsules' `0x045A` follows `0x9007` / `0x9008` *(captured, capture-validation §19.6, §24.4, §24.5)*; flows §3.14, §3.32 | D03 §4, D02 §7.2 |
| character logout | D→G `0x040B NC_LOG_GAME_LOGOUT` (G FUN_004033d0; a copy of the WM's `0x1037`, 4 ms after it: money and position are the WM cache's stale copy, not the logout state ((original bug) P02-B36) *(captured, capture-validation §22.5, §23.5)*); W→G `0xC807` (G FUN_00406fe0); W→AL `0x081F NC_MISC_CHAR_LOGOFF_STATISTICS` (AL FUN_00402990), `0x0843 NC_MISC_EVENT_LONG_TIME_PLAY_CMD` after ≥ 60 min (AL FUN_004030d0) | D02 §5, D01 §4.2 |
| WM session closed | W→AL `0x0722 NC_LOG_USER_LOGOUT` (AL FUN_00402640, type 2), `0xC803` (AL FUN_004034e0); W→A `0x0C11` (no-op) | D01 §3.1, §4.1, §4.5 |
| user count, every minute | AL→every WM `0x0809 NC_MISC_CONNECTER_REQ` (`CStatistics::Process` FUN_004064b0; payload byte from a reused pointer, P02-B6) → W FUN_00408f50 → AL `0xC811 NC_SYSLOG_SERVER_CCU` and `0x080A NC_MISC_CONNECTER_ACK` (AL FUN_004026f0), in the same ms or one WM pass later (0–15 ms) *(captured, capture-validation §7.2)* | D08 §3.3, D01 §4.2, W01 §8 |
| Character → GameLog records | the Character server's records (`0x040A`, `0x040B`, `0x0412`, item logs) go over its own GameLog link; records produced before GameLog's `0x0803` are dropped | D08 §4.3 |
| periodic link traffic (not logs) | W→L `0x0808 NC_MISC_USER_COUNT_CMD` every 30.0 s (the first `{0, 0}`, P02-B22, §3.2); W→every Z `0x0821` every 60 s → the zone's per-minute block; `0x7495` per minute and `0x987A` / `0x987B` at :00 on the zone links *(captured, capture-validation §7.2, §7.5.3, §7.5.4)* | W01 §2, §8, Z09 §1.2 |

### 3.16 Chat routing

Map chat stays in the zone; everything addressed to a person or a group goes through the WM, which has every
in-game session (W04 §2). No chat message reaches the Character server or GameLog.

| Kind | Sequence (receiver handler) | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| say | C→Z `0x2001 NC_ACT_CHAT_REQ` (Z FUN_0045a400) → clients in sight | Z02 §3.1 |
| shout | C→Z `0x201E NC_ACT_SHOUT_CMD` (Z FUN_00458e80; level > `shoutlevel` of the WM's `0x0830`, delay) → `0x201F` to the whole map instance (`so_AllInMap1`), not to other maps or zones. *(captured)*: the shouter gets `0x201F` too, a level-1 shout is dropped silently, flag bits 2–7 are stale ((original bug) P02-B29) | Z02 §3.2; capture-validation §18.4 |
| whisper | C→W `0x200C NC_ACT_WHISPER_REQ` (W FUN_0041c610; prison, arena, length checks) → target C `0x200D NC_ACT_SOMEONEWHISPER_CMD` (client FUN_004a9c80, drops it when the talker is on the client's block list), sender C `0x200F` / `0x200E` (client FUN_004a9ea0); W→sender's Z `0x6006 NC_CT_CHARTTING_CMD` (Z FUN_005a3ce0, chat-title counter). *(captured)*: 11 ms after `0x200C` the receiver's `0x200D`, the sender's `0x200F` and `0x6006` (on the zone's broadcast link) leave in one ms; `0x200D`'s flag bits 1–7 are stack bytes (W04 §2.1, linked to P02-B29) | W04 §2.1, Z08 §7.2; capture-validation §18.4 |
| party chat | C→W `0x2014 NC_ACT_PARTYCHAT_REQ` (W FUN_00413510) → every confirmed online member (sender too) `0x2015` (client FUN_004a1890), sender `0x2016` (client FUN_004a9f00); W→Z `0x6006` | W04 §2.3 |
| raid chat, raid warning | C→W `0xB03F` / `0xB042` (W FUN_00417630) → raid members | W02 §5.12 |
| guild chat | C→W `0x7473 NC_GUILD_CHAT_REQ` (W FUN_00415230) → online guild members | W03 §3.6 |
| notice | C (admin level 100 only) / Z / OPTool →W `0x2010 NC_ACT_NOTICE_REQ` (W FUN_00413450 / FUN_0042a920 / FUN_00425c90) → every client session `0x2011 NC_ACT_NOTICE_CMD` (client FUN_004b5420, game scene only); stack overflow above 131 bytes, W04-B1 | W04 §2.2 |
| world script message | Z (script engine) →W `0x207D NC_ACT_SCRIPT_MSG_WORLD_CMD` (W FUN_0042cea0) → every in-game client `0x2079 NC_ACT_SCRIPT_MSG_CMD` (client FUN_004bc020), unchanged 6 ms later (Z09-B72) *(captured, capture-validation §20.4)* | W04 §2.4, Z15 |
| level announcement | Z→W `0x6401 NC_ANNOUNCE_Z2W_CMD` (W FUN_0042ab50; `&levelup` past level 20) → every client `0x6402 NC_ANNOUNCE_W2C_CMD` 88 ms later (Z11-B30); the title marks D `0x6005` + C `0x6004` come before the level's `0x240C` *(captured, capture-validation §20.4)* | W01 §7.6, W04 §6.2, Z11 §7.3 |
| chat ban, spam | the WM's `singleOption.nChatBan` turns whisper and party chat into a silent echo for the sender; the zone's own spam filter (Z02 §3.4) and the WM's gold-spammer report and GM chat ban (`0x085F` … `0x0869`) | W04 §2.1, §2.5, §17.2, §17.4 |
| block list | C→W dept 42 `NC_CHAT_RESTRICT_*` → D (D04 §10) → W → C; the list is applied by the client | W04 §14 |

### 3.17 Party

The WM owns the party registry (W02 §1.2); every zone keeps a mirror fed by **broadcasts to all zones** (W02 §8), and
each zone sends the party status packets of its own players (Z08 §1.2).

**a) Invite and accept:**

| # | Hop | Message | Handler | What happens | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | C→W | `0x3802 NC_PARTY_JOIN_REQ {target}` | W FUN_0041a940 | checks (size, prison, map `canparty`, guild-only map, KQ team); both records pending; refusals `0x3807 {err}` | W02 §2.1 |
| 2 | W→C | `0x3803 NC_PARTY_JOINPROPOSE_REQ {mastername}` | client FUN_004a29d0 | invitation dialog | W02 §2.1 |
| 3 | C→W | `0x3804 NC_PARTY_JOINPROPOSE_ALLOW_ACK` (or `0x3805` reject) | W FUN_004211e0 | confirm the member | W02 §2.2, §2.3 |
| 4 | W→every Z | `0x3801 NC_PARTY_FUNDAMENTAL_CMD` (party born) or `0x3808 NC_PARTY_JOIN_CMD` | Z FUN_004cd5c0 / FUN_004cd730 | zone mirror created / member added (`partyregnum < 4000`) | Z08 §1.3 |
| 5 | W→C | members `0x3807 {name, class, 0x4C1}` (client FUN_004ac300); joiner member list `0x3809`, looter info | | friends get `0x540E` (§3.22) | W02 §2.2 |
| 6 | C→Z | `0x3848 NC_PARTY_MEMBERINFOREQ_CMD` | Z FUN_004cd820 | resets the members' send cache: the player tick (`sp_SendMyInform2PartyMember` FUN_0056df80) resends `0x3832` / `0x3833` / `0x3849` to the members on **this** zone | Z08 §1.2, §1.3 |

**b) Other changes**: zone-driven join without invitation Z→W `0x3860 NC_PARTY_ZONE_JOIN_CMD` (W FUN_0042e8c0; refusals
only logged, guild-map check ignored W02-B11) then steps 4–5; leave C→W `0x380A` (W FUN_0041b990) or zone-driven
`0x3861` (W FUN_0042cbf0) → all Z `0x380C NC_PARTY_LEAVE_CMD` (Z FUN_004cda00), C `0x380B`; kick `0x3814` → all Z
`0x3816` (Z FUN_004cdb20); a 2-member party dissolves: all Z `0x381F NC_PARTY_DISMISS_CMD` (Z FUN_004cc060: the party's
instances are deleted, §3.24), members `0x381E`; master, loot rule and looter: `0x382A`, `0x384D`, `0x385E` (Z08 §1.3).

**c) Session hooks**: map login (W `0x180C`, §3.9) → `Client_PartyRefreshOnMapLogin` FUN_00430a30 refreshes the
record's handles, leaves on a party-release map, sends the member list, all Z `0x383C` (empty handler in the zone) and
members `0x383D`. Logout (`CharacterLogout`, §3.12) → `Client_PartyOnLogout` FUN_00436670: all Z `0x3846` (empty
handler), members `0x3847`; with ≤ 1 member online the party is released (`0x381F`); otherwise the member stays in the
party while offline. Zone resync: Z→W `0x384E NC_PARTY_MEMBERINFORM_REQ` (W FUN_0042d5a0; also from the OPTool, W
FUN_004274e0) → that zone `0x384F` (Z FUN_004cd4b0, rebuilds the mirror when the list differs). Party experience and
loot are zone-local (Z29, Z09).

### 3.19 Guild

The guild data lives in the Character DB and is loaded by the WM at start-up (W03 §9.1); the WM answers the clients
and broadcasts a reduced mirror (`GUILD_ZONE`) to every zone (W03 §10). Guild storage is a zone ↔ Character server
path with only the member grade asked from the WM.

| Step | Sequence (receiver handler) | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| create | C→W `0x7405 NC_GUILD_MAKE_REQ` (W FUN_0041d380 → `Guild_MakeReq` FUN_0044cf10: level 20, name) → D `0x7458 NC_GUILD_DB_MAKE_REQ` (D FUN_00438440; item and money refusals come from here) → W `0x7459` (W FUN_0040b9d0) → C `0x7406` (client FUN_006b7aa0), members `0x7436`; all Z `0x7448 NC_GUILD_ZONE_MAKE_CMD` (Z FUN_005a40d0) | W03 §2.2, D04 §6.2, Z08 §8.2 |
| invite, join | C→W `0x7409 NC_GUILD_MEMBER_INVITE_REQ` (W FUN_0041d450) → invitee; answer C→W `0x740C NC_GUILD_MEMBER_JOIN_ACK` (W FUN_00419db0) → D `0x7469` (D FUN_00415270; academy member: `0x982F`, §3.20) → W `0x746A` (W FUN_0040bbd0) → members `0x7436`, all Z `0x744A` (Z FUN_005a4230); refusals `0x740D` (client FUN_006b11a0); a refused invitation is not reported to the inviter; a Character-DB link that is down (or a failed write) gives the invitee `0x740D {0x0CDB}` after the invite was cleared | W03 §2.4, §2.5, W06 §2.1 |
| leave, kick, grade, notice | C→W `0x741C` (W FUN_00415070) → D `0x746B` → W `0x746C` (W FUN_0040bc20); kick `0x740E` (W FUN_0041d6f0) → D `0x7471` → W `0x7472` (W FUN_0040bcc0); grade `0x7416`, notice `0x7410` (W03 §2.8, §2.9); members told, leave / kick: all Z `0x744B` (Z FUN_005a4340) | W03 §2.6–§2.9, D04 §6.3 |
| map login, logout, level, rename / delete of a character | W `0x180C` → `Client_RefreshGuildState` FUN_004360f0; `Guild_OnLogout` FUN_0044ccf0; Z→W `0x1074` (W FUN_0042e160); `CharIDChange` FUN_00431dd0 | W03 §4 |
| war | C→W `0x7422 NC_GUILD_WAR_REQ` (W FUN_00414e50 → FUN_0044dba0); WM tick `Guilds_WarTick` FUN_0044ce00 → all Z `0x7443` / `0x7444` (Z FUN_005a3ea0 / FUN_005a3f60); kills Z→W `0x7445 NC_GUILD_ZONE_WAR_KILL_CMD` (W FUN_0042ac50) → both guilds' members `0x742A` (no 2016 client handler); every error reply to the war-able list request C→W `0x7424` (W FUN_004151b0 → FUN_0043e270; answer W→C `0x7425`, client FUN_006b3e00) also empties the client's list (W06-B4), codes 0x0D1A / 0x0D1C show the client's default line (W06-B3, B2) | W03 §3.2–§3.4, W06 §2.2 |
| storage open | C→Z `0x7491 NC_GUILD_STORAGEOPEN_REQ` (Z FUN_004762d0) → D `0x3471 NC_ITEMDB_OPEN_GUILD_STORAGE_REQ` (D FUN_0040dd30) → Z `0x3473` (Z FUN_00475b20) → C `0x7492`; in parallel Z→W `0x749E NC_GUILD_STORAGE_WITHDRAW_GRADE_REQ` (W FUN_0042b0c0) → Z `0x749F` (Z FUN_005a48f0; error replies have no routing header, W03-B17) | Z08 §8.1, Z05 §7.2, W03 §9.5, D03 §5.2 |
| storage withdraw | C→Z `0x3026 NC_ITEM_GUILD_STORAGE_WITHDRAW_REQ` (Z FUN_00537330) → D `0x3474` (D FUN_00411460) → Z `0x3475` (Z FUN_005347a0) → C `0x3027`; Z→next Z (ring) `0x7493 NC_GUILD_STORAGEWITHDRAW_RNG` (Z FUN_00474f10, not forwarded further: Z14-B10) → guild members on that zone and on the origin zone `0x7494` (client FUN_006b4880) | Z05 §7.2, Z14 §3 |
| rename, retype (items) | C→Z `0x74AC NC_GUILD_RENAME_REQ` (Z FUN_00473550, item in a slot) → W (master, war, tournament and name checks) → D `0x74B2 NC_GUILD_DB_RENAME_REQ` → W `0x74B3` (W FUN_00410890); W→asking Z `0x74B0` (retype `0x74B8`), every in-game client `0x74AE NC_GUILD_RENAME_CMD`; the asking zone (Z FUN_005a4ad0 / FUN_005a4bf0 → `so_ply_GuildReName` FUN_00474650 / ReType) repeats the checks, consumes the item through ITEMDB and keeps the new name in a 128-entry queue until the DB confirms; errors C `0x74AD` / `0x74B5` | W03 §9.3, §9.4, Z08 §8.1, Z31 §2.1 |

### 3.23 Kingdom quest

The WM keeps the schedule and the joiner lists (`CKQ`, W04 §6.1); the zone that hosts the KQ maps runs them (Z16).

| # | Hop | Message | Handler | What happens | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | W | `KQ_Tick` FUN_00456190 (1 s) | | `KingdomQuest.shn` rows → next two start times; at start time free map slots are taken | W04 §6.2 |
| 2 | W→every Z | `0x580D NC_KQ_W2Z_MAKE_REQ {PROTO_KQ_INFO}` | Z FUN_00497790 | every zone builds the element and marks the map instances it hosts | Z16 §2.1 |
| 3 | Z→W | `0x580E NC_KQ_Z2W_MAKE_ACK {hquest, err 0x981}` | W FUN_0042a9c0 | sent only by a zone that hosts the **last** listed map (Z16-B2); other errors → Status 8; Status 2 and a joining alarm to every client session; no answer → 1-min timeout, Status 9 | W04 §6.3, Z16 §2.1 |
| 4 | C→W | `0x581B NC_KQ_LIST_REFRESH_REQ`, `0x5805 NC_KQ_JOIN_REQ`, `0x5807` cancel | W FUN_00413f80 / FUN_00413cc0 / FUN_00413d70 | list diffs `0x581C` … `0x581F` (client FUN_0049add0 …); join `0x5806`; cancel → all Z `0x583B` (Z FUN_00499100) | W04 §6.4 |
| 5 | W | start wait over, enough joiners, teams | | 10-s countdown (notice `0x580B` to the joiners, `0x5825` each second); then the joiners leave their parties / raids (§3.17) | W04 §6.2 |
| 6 | W→every Z | `0x580F NC_KQ_W2Z_START_CMD {info, joiners}` | Z FUN_00499240 | the zone that hosts map 0 starts the map script and inserts the joiners into the KQ's player list (`kqe_PlayerList`); **every** zone also copies the joiners into an entrance slot (`KingdomQuestEntrance::operator[]` FUN_00496150, outside the map-0 test; re-read in part 8) and runs gate-keeper events at 0, 2, 5, 7, 10 s (`erb_happen` FUN_00496280) that look its own players up in that slot (`kqe_search`, not the player list) and link them to map 0 (`so_LinkTo` → §3.11: another zone when map 0 is not local), logging `0x0441 NC_LOG_GAME_KQ_ENTER` to G. So joiners on any zone are pulled in by their own zone | Z16 §2.2, §2.3 |
| 7 | C→Z | `0x581A NC_KQ_ENTRYRESPONCE_ACK` | Z FUN_00496990 | links to map 0 without a joiner check (Z16-B4) | Z16 §2.3 |
| 8 | W→Z | `0x5833 NC_KQ_LINK_TO_FORCE_BY_BAN_CMD` (vote ban) | Z FUN_00497120 | the banned player is linked to Roumen | W04 §6.5, Z16 §2.5 |
| 9 | Z→W | `0x5810 NC_KQ_Z2W_END_CMD` (script `cEndOfKingdomQuest`) | W FUN_0042aac0 | Status 5 → all Z `0x5811 NC_KQ_W2Z_DESTROY_CMD` (Z FUN_004972c0: maps cleared; players left on them go to Roumen at their next map login or revive). `0x5811` also when the WM cancels (too few joiners, `SetDoneSkip` FUN_004548c0) | W04 §6.3, Z16 §2.5 |
| 10 | Z→D | `0x5815 NC_KQ_REWARD_REQ {fame, cen, item list}` | D FUN_004321d0 | → Z `0x5816` / `0x5817` (Z FUN_0052e570 / FUN_0052e680) | Z16 §3.4, D04 §3 |

A KQ skipped at its start minute (hh:mm:00) *(captured, capture-validation §20.4)*: `0x5811` to each zone, `0x5825` to each client, then `0x581E` to one client per second.

| gate on the hosting zone | C→Z NPC menu (IDGate) → `cm_PlayerinToInstanceDungeon` → `so_LinkTo` (§3.11) | Z08 §5.2, Z14 §1.4 |
| gate on another zone | Z→ring `0xA404 NC_INSTANCE_DUNGEON_FIND_RNG` (each Z FUN_00487510; the hosting zone takes an instance, the answer travels on to the origin) → origin `so_LinkTo` (no `sp_LinkReplyCheck`, Z14-B8) | Z14 §2.1 |
| level select (ModeIDGate) | Z→W `0xA408` (W FUN_0042c630) → destination Z `0xA40A` (Z FUN_00486ff0) → W `0xA40B` (W FUN_0042c7d0) → origin Z `0xA409` (Z FUN_00485540); then Z→W `0xA40E` (W FUN_0042c8e0) → destination Z `0xA410` (Z FUN_004871a0) → W `0xA411` (W FUN_0042cac0) → origin Z `0xA40F` (Z FUN_00485b90) → `so_LinkTo` | W04 §13.2, Z08 §5.3 |
| empty countdown | Z (`mc_Routine` FUN_004868d0, 20 s empty) →W `0xA401` (W FUN_0042ba20) → party / raid members `0x0810 NC_MISC_RESTMINUTE_CMD {1, 300}` (client FUN_004997a0), all Z `0xA402` (Z FUN_00484590: the leader's zone opens the "delete now" menu); players back or instance deleted → Z→W `0xA403` (W FUN_0042bc40) → members `0x0810 {1, 0}` | W04 §13.1, Z14 §1.6 |
| delete everywhere | leader's "delete now" → ring `0x10B1 NC_CHAR_EMPTY_INSTANCE_DUNGEON_RNG` (Z FUN_00485120); party / raid dismissal (`0x381F`, `0xB007`) and guild deletion delete the group's instances on every zone | Z14 §1.7, §2.2 |
| job-change dungeon | ring `0x441F NC_QUEST_JOBDUNGEON_FIND_RNG` (Z FUN_005bfde0) | Z14 §2.3 |
| ranking | W `g_IndunRank` | W04 §13.3 |

### 3.25 Trade and booths

Both partners of a trade and a booth's seller and buyer are on the same map *(inferred: they are map objects of one
zone)*; the zone reserves the cells and money (lock list) and the Character server moves the rows in one request.

| Step | Sequence (receiver handler) | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| trade proposal, board, lock | C→Z dept 19 `NC_TRADE_*` (zone only) | Z06 §1.2, §1.3 |
| trade commit | C→Z `0x4C1F NC_TRADE_DECIDE_REQ` (Z FUN_00594870), both decided → both C `0x4C24 NC_TRADE_TRADECOMPLETE_CMD` **before** the DB answer (Z06-B15) → D `0x343B NC_ITEMDB_ITEMTRADE_REQ` (D FUN_0040ca80: money, then items; undo on failure) → Z `0x343D` / `0x343C` per partner (Z FUN_00593ed0 / FUN_00593dd0: apply or free, `0x4C23` on failure); D→G `0x0460` / `0x0461` / `0x044A` | Z06 §1.4, D03 §14, Z05 §9.10 |
| booth open, visit | C→Z dept 26 (zone only) | Z06 §2.2–§2.4 |
| booth purchase / sale | C→Z `0x680D NC_BOOTH_ITEMTRADE_REQ` (Z FUN_0058da30) → D `0x3453` / `0x3454` / `0x3455` (D FUN_0040f510 / FUN_0040f950 / FUN_0040feb0, whole stack / merge / split) → Z `0x3456 NC_ITEMDB_BOOTHTRADE_ACK` (Z FUN_005914a0: both locks; buyer `0x680E` on failure), owner `0x680F`; D→G `0x045B` / `0x045C` | Z06 §2.5, D03 §14, Z05 §9.11 |
| market search | zone only | Z06 §2.6 |

### 3.26 Premium items and buffs

| Feature | Sequence (receiver handler) | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| item-shop purchases | C→Z `0x3020 NC_ITEM_CHARGEDINVENOPEN_REQ` (Z FUN_004923c0) → D `0x3463 NC_ITEMDB_CHARGED_LIST_REQ` (D FUN_004116e0; the procedure reads `Account.dbo.tChargeItem` by a cross-database call, not by a server link) → Z `0x3464` (Z FUN_00492570) → C `0x3021` | D03 §5.4, Z05 §7.4 |

### 3.27 Prison

| Path | Sequence (receiver handler) | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| imprison an online player | GM C→W `0x7C01 NC_PRISON_ADD_GM_REQ` (W FUN_0041de70; any admin level) → target's Z `0x7C13 NC_ZONE_PRISON_GO_REQ` (Z FUN_005a4470: link to `EldPri`; err 0xDC0 ok, 0xDC1 other charNo at the handle, 0xDC2 failed; no answer when the handle is unknown or empty, X01-B4) → W `0x7C14` (W FUN_0042dea0: timer `CPrisonServer_Add`) → D `0x7C0B` (D FUN_00436df0) → W `0x7C0C` (W FUN_00410a50: `prisonmin`, leaves the KQ list) → GM `0x7C02` | W04 §9.2, §9.3, Z08 §12, D04 §7 |
| imprison an offline player | W → D `0x7C0B` directly (by name) → W `0x7C0C` → GM `0x7C02` | W04 §9.2 |
| map login in prison | Z → C `0x7C05` (minutes·60) at the `EldPri` map login; C→W `0x7C06 NC_PRISON_GET_REQ` (W FUN_00415530) → D `0x7C0E` (D FUN_004166d0) → W `0x7C0F` (W FUN_0040c350: timer starts) → C `0x7C07` | W04 §9.2, §9.3, Z01 |
| serving time | W `CPrisonServer_Tick` FUN_00458360 (2 s) → D `0x7C0D` (D FUN_00416650) every 3 minutes; at 0: C `0x7C05 {0x0DA8}`, Z `0x7C12 NC_ZONE_PRISON_END_CMD` (Z FUN_005a60f0), D `0x7C0D {0}`; C→W `0x7C04` (W FUN_004155f0) asks early (milliseconds as seconds, W04-B24); logout saves the minutes (`CPrisonServer_SaveOnLogout` FUN_00458790) | W04 §9.1, §9.2 |
| change, release | GM C→W `0x7C09` (W FUN_0041e290; minutes and reason never copied, W04-B25) → D `0x7C10` (D FUN_00437010) → W `0x7C11` (W FUN_00410b90) → prisoner `0x7C03` or release, GM `0x7C0A` | W04 §9.2, §9.3 |

### 3.28 GM commands and OPTool across processes

| Action | Sequence (receiver handler) | Docs |
|---|---|---|
| OPTool kick by user number | OPTool→W `0x2829 NC_OPTOOL_KICK_USER_REQ` (W FUN_00426e20) → every Z `0x0C16` (Z FUN_005a5f90 → `so_banning`, save, §3.12 f), C `0x0C17 {0x068F}`, close reason 0x790; OPTool `0x282A`; find only: `0x2827` (W FUN_00426c50) | W04 §4.3 |
| OPTool character delete | OPTool→W `0x281D` (playing?) and `0x281F` (W FUN_00426730 → `Clients_OnCharacterDeleted` FUN_004382d0) → friends `0x540C`, guild members `0x7438`, D `0x9424` (D FUN_0041a4f0, holy promise), all Z `0x10B2 NC_CHAR_WEDDING_CANCEL_WEDDING` (Z FUN_005a07d0), academy `0x9860`; the WM deletes no DB row | W04 §4.3 |
| OPTool at Login, zones, DataServers | dept 10 status, user limits, kicks, notices on each process's OPTool listener; `0x2803 NC_OPTOOL_CLOSE_SERVER_REQ` → `0x2804`, then the DataServer stops within a second (D08-B2; unauthenticated, D05-B7) | `login-server.md` §5.4, Z01 §3.3, D01 §2, D05 §5, D08 §1.3 |
| OPTool kingdom quest, guild, tournament | OPTool→W `0x2811` … (W04 §4.2, §4.4) | W04 §4.2, §4.4 |
| GM events | OPTool→W `0xD001` … `0xD009` (`GMEventManager`) → all Z `0xD00B NC_EVENT_ADD_UPDATE_EVENT_CMD` / `0xD00C NC_EVENT_DEL_EVENT_CMD` (Z FUN_0046ddf0 / FUN_0046dae0); a deleted active event is not withdrawn (W04-B50) | W04 §16, Z08 §13 |
| `&pfind`, `&psummon` | GM chat line → Z (`ac_FindPlayer` FUN_0041d050 / `ac_SummonPlayer` FUN_0041d140) → local, else ring `0x1068 NC_CHAR_PLAYERSEARCH_RNG` (Z FUN_0045b870) → `0x1069` (Z FUN_0045ba60) back to the origin (the GM links to the player) / `0x106A NC_CHAR_PLAYERSUMMON_RNG` (Z FUN_0045b700: the target links to the GM); every move is a map change (§3.11) | Z11 §5.1, Z01 §10 |
| kick through the ring | ring `0x106B NC_CHAR_KICKPLAYEROUT_RNG` (Z FUN_0045bc30: `so_Disconnect(0x68B)` → W `0x0C1A`, §3.12 f) → `0x106C` (Z FUN_0045bdf0) to the origin | Z01 §10 |
| `&expbonus A`, `&itembonus A` | ring `0x1810` / `0x1811` (Z FUN_0045bf30 / FUN_0045bff0) → every map of every zone; the EXP value has no reader (Z11-B15) | Z11 §5.4 |
| ring test | `&` command → ring `0x0811 NC_MISC_ZONERINGLINKTEST_RNG` (Z FUN_0045b4d0) round the ring | Z01 §10 |
| notices | §3.16 | W04 §2.2 |
| `&hide`, `&show`, `&dimension` | inside one zone: §3.31 *(captured)* | Z11 §3.1 |

Prison is §3.27. The other `&` commands act inside one zone (Z11 §2) or reach another zone only through a map change.
